def update_c_pair(a, c, i, j, delta):

Smooth objective function `smooth_obj(x, n, alpha)` using FFT correlation:
```
def correlation_counts(x,n):
    # x length n
    # c_counts = conv(x, (1-x)[::-1]) length 2n-1
    L=1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()
    F=x=np.fft.rfft(x, L)
    G=np.fft.rfft((1-x)[::-1], L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*G, L)[:2*n-1]
    return c
```
Need maybe remove tiny imaginary. `np.fft` available. For n<= maybe 2048. The output might have numerical error ~1e-12. Fine.
For alpha LSE:
```

mx = z.max()
lse = (mx + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z-mx))))/alpha
val = m + lse  # since lse approx? Actually logsumexp(alpha(c-m)) = mx + log sum exp(z-mx). f = m + (that)/alpha.
weights? Not needed if only value.
```
But value for line search should be smooth f = logsumexp(alpha c)/alpha. Using m offset: = m + (mx + logsumexp shifted)/alpha. If alpha large, shifted max 0.
Gradient:
```
def smooth_grad(x,n,alpha):
 c=...; m=c.max(); z=alpha*(c-m); mx=z.max(); s=np.sum(np.exp(z-mx)); p=np.exp(z-mx)/s
 return weighted_gradient(p,x,n)
```

```
def fft_convolve(a,b):
    L=1; while L < len(a)+len(b)-1: L*=2
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L); fb=np.fft.rfft(b,L); return np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,L)[:len(a)+len(b)-1]
```
For weighted_grad, B=1-x, W=p (or p maybe real). A_rev=x[::-1]. Use two FFTs maybe. `fft_convolve(B,p)` and `fft_convolve(x[::-1],p)`. n up to 1000, per eval 2 inverse FFTs. Could combine using complex? Not necessary.

Let's refine algorithms.

Exact evaluation:
```
def evaluate_exact(h):
    n=len(h); dx=2.0/n
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full') * dx
    return float(np.max(c))
```
For many evaluations in coarse DE use direct np.correlate (M small). For large use FFT but max same; for final use np.

Projection:
```
def project_feasible(y, n):
    S = 0.5*n
    y = np.asarray(y, float)
    # if close feasible, return
    lo = y.min() - 1.0
    hi = y.max()
    # ensure sum at lo >=S? At tau=lo, x=1 => sum n; at hi, 0 =>0.
    for _ in range(60):
       tau=0.5*(lo+hi)
       x=np.clip(y-tau,0,1)
       s=x.sum()
       if s > S: tau = tau + 1e-? need sum decrease as tau increases. If s > S, increase tau (lo=tau)
       else: hi=tau
    x=np.clip(y-0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
    # adjust residual in a coordinate to make sum exact? Due bisection error ~1e-15
    diff=S-x.sum()
    if diff !=0:
       # add to entries with room if diff>0 else subtract
       if diff>0:
          room=1-x; idx=argmax(room?) distribute all to one? Could break bounds if room <diff (shouldn't if tau not extreme). Use top few.
          # simpler: x += diff/n? may violate bounds slightly. Accept floating tolerance.
       ...
    return x
```

To be safe, implement `fix_sum` that adds residual to variable with max slack if positive, else min, individually capped; if residual remains, distribute. O(n). 
```
def fix_sum(x, n):
  S=n/2; diff=S-x.sum()
  if abs(diff)>1e-14:
    if diff>0:
       slack=1-x; idxs=np.argsort(-slack)[:max(1,int(abs(diff)))?]
       # if one var slack enough, adjust it; else distribute
       for idx in idxs:
         add=min(slack[idx], diff); x[idx]+=add; diff-=add
    else similar
  return x
```
But if bisection gave sum off by 1e-13 and max slack enough, one adjust. Good.

FFT functions:
```
def _fft_len(N): return 1 << (N-1).bit_length()
def fft_convolve(a,b):
    n=len(a); m=len(b); L=1<<(n+m-2).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,L); B=np.fft.rfft(b,L)
    return np.fft.irfft(A*B,L)[:n+m-1]
def correlation_counts_fft(x):
    n=x.shape[0]
    return fft_convolve(x, (1.0-x)[::-1])
```
Could cache Fourier of (1-x) reversed? x changes each step.

```
def obj_alpha(x,n,alpha):
   dx=2.0/n
   c=correlation_counts_fft(x)*dx
   m=np.max(c)
   z=alpha*(c-m)
   mx=np.max(z)
   # if alpha small and many terms, sum exp maybe okay because z<=0
   return float(m + (mx + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z-mx))))/alpha)
```
If alpha=0? Use max? We'll use alpha>0.
Gradient:
```
def grad_alpha(x,n,alpha):
   c=correlation_counts_fft(x)*dx
   m=c.max(); z=alpha*(c-m); mx=z.max(); e=np.exp(z-mx); p=e/e.sum()
   return weighted_grad_counts(p,x,n)*dx
def weighted_grad_counts(p,x,n):
   # p length 2n-1
   a=x; b=1-a; arev=a[::-1]
   g1=fft_convolve(b,p)[n-1:2*n-1]
   g2=fft_convolve(arev,p)[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
   return g1-g2
```
This uses two fft convolve. Need p real. For n maybe large, memory. Good.

Implement:
```
def update_c_from(a, brev, c, i, j, delta):
    n = a.shape[0]
    # point i +delta
    c[i:i+n] += delta * brev
    s = n-1-i
    c[s:s+n] -= delta * a
    # point j -delta (val = -delta)
    c[j:j+n] += (-delta) * brev
    s = n-1-j
    c[s:s+n] -= (-delta) * a  # i.e. += delta*a
    # quadratic:
    # i with i: p=n-1, val d, val_rev d => c[n-1] -= d*d
    # i with j: p=i+n-1-j, d * (-d) => c[p] -= -d^2 = +d^2
    # j with i: p=j+n-1-i, (-d)*d = +d^2
    # j with j: p=n-1, (-d)*(-d)=d^2 -> c[n-1] -= d^2
    c[n-1] -= 2*delta*delta
    p1 = i + n - 1 - j
    if p1 != n-1: c[p1] += delta*delta
    p2 = j + n - 1 - i
    if p2 != n-1: c[p2] += delta*delta
```
But careful: In c[j:j+n] += -delta*brev, for j index, if i+j? okay. The linear b change for j with val=-delta: s2=n-1-j, c[s2:s2+n] -= val*a = -(-delta)*a = +delta*a. Yes.
Does update formula with `brev` include brev for point i as old, which if point j? If m-d = j, brev old, quadratic covers. Good.

```
def linearize_lp_step(h, n, max_it=20, deadline):
   for it:
     c = correlate(h,1-h)
     active = top lags (c >= max - tol)
     # compute gradients for active lags directly via loops? Or use weighted_grad_counts with uniform weights active.
     # Build affine coefficients for t constraints for active lags only: c_l + grad_l·(h - h0) <= t.

`def project_feasible(y,n):` as above. Need top-level. Use bisection and fix.
Detailed:
```
def project_feasible(y, n):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
    if y.shape[0] != n: y = np.resize? But assume.
    S = 0.5 * n
    # if close and bounds, return with fix
    if y.min() >= -1e-12 and y.max() <= 1+1e-12:
        x = np.clip(y,0,1)
        if abs(x.sum()-S) < 1e-12:
            return x
    lo = y.min() - 1.0 - 1e-9
    hi = y.max() + 1e-9
    # ensure at lo sum n, hi sum0
    # 60 iterations
    for _ in range(60):
       tau = 0.5*(lo+hi)
       s = np.clip(y - tau, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
       if s > S:
          lo = tau
       else:
          hi = tau
    x = np.clip(y - 0.5*(lo+hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    return _fix_sum(x,n)
```
`_fix_sum`:
```
def _fix_sum(x,n):
 S=0.5*n; diff=S-x.sum()
 if abs(diff) <= 1e-13: return x
 if diff >0:
   idx=np.argmax(1.0-x)
   add=min(diff,1.0-x[idx])
   if add>0: x[idx]+=add; diff-=add
   if diff>1e-15:
      # distribute over top 100
      idxs=np.argsort(1.0-x)[::-1]
      for k in idxs:
        if diff <=1e-15: break
        a=min(diff,1.0-x[k]); x[k]+=a; diff-=a
 else:
   idx=np.argmax(x)
   sub=min(-diff,x[idx]); if sub>0: x[idx]-=sub; diff+=sub
   if diff < -1e-15:
      idxs=np.argsort(x)[:...]? for min x
      for k in idxs:
         if diff >=-1e-15: break
         a=min(-diff,x[k]); x[k]-=a; diff+=a
 x=np.clip(x,0,1)
 # final if still diff due clipping, adjust same
 return x
```
But changing one value may break if diff large from bad tau; projection bisection should not. Good.

```
def try_pair_move(a,brev,c,i,j,delta):
    if delta == 0: return None
    # check bounds with current a
    if i==j: return None
    n=a.shape[0]
    # For direction +delta to i, - to j:
    if delta > 0:
       if a[i] + delta > 1+1e-12 or a[j] - delta < -1e-12: return None
    else:
       if a[i] - delta < -1e-12 or a[j] + delta > 1+1e-12: return None
    _update_pair_counts(a,brev,c,i,j,delta)
    a[i] += delta; a[j] -= delta
    bi=n-1-i; bj=n-1-j
    brev[bi] -= delta; brev[bj] += delta
    newc = float(c.max())
    return newc
def revert_pair_move(a,brev,c,i,j,delta):
    # state currently after +delta; revert with -delta using current a,brev
    _update_pair_counts(a,brev,c,i,j,-delta)
    a[i] -= delta; a[j] += delta
    bi=n-1-i; bj=n-1-j
    brev[bi] += delta; brev[bj] -= delta
```

def subgradient_polish(h,n,deadline,rng, max_iters=200):
   h=project_feasible(h,n)
   c=exact counts via np.correlate; best=...
   v = zeros(n)
   for it in range(max_iters):
     if time > deadline: break
     # active weights top K with weights proportional to softmax of c - max + tol
     idx = np.argpartition(c, -active_k)[-active_k:]
     w = zeros(2n-1)
     vals = c[idx] - cmax
     probs = exp(alpha_sg*vals) alpha_sg maybe 50/dx? Actually vals negative small; use softmax over top with temperature =1e-4 maybe counts.
     w[idx]=probs/probs.sum
     g = weighted_gradient_counts(w,h,n)*(2/n)
     d = -g; d -= d.mean()
     # momentum
     v = 0.8*v + d
     d = v
     # scale: if max|d| > 1? We'll line search using steps
     norm = np.max(np.abs(d))
     if norm < 1e-12: continue
     d = d / norm
     # trial steps [100,50,20,10,5,2,1,0.5,0.2,0.1]
     best_trial=None
     for step in steps:
        y = project_feasible(h + step*d, n)
        val = exact C via counts max*dx
        if val < best - 1e-14: accept; break
     if not accept: maybe try smaller; if no, reduce temperature? 
     # if no improvement several, break
   return h,best
```

Subgradient implementation optional. Let's design simple and deadline-safe:
```
def subgradient_polish(h,n,deadline,rng, max_iters=80):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
    best_count=float(c.max()*(2/n))
    best_h=h.copy()
    v=np.zeros(n)
    stall=0
    alpha_w=50.0  # for weights top lags? counts differences
    active_k=7
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.time()>deadline-2 or no improve > 30: break
       cmax = float(c.max())
       # weights: use all lags with threshold top? choose top active_k, softmax with temp 1e-4? c differences maybe small. Use softmax = exp((c[idx]-cmax)*alpha_w)
       idx = np.argpartition(c, -active_k)[-active_k:]
       wcnt = np.exp((c[idx]-cmax)*alpha_w)
       if wcnt.sum() <=0: wcnt=np.ones_like(wcnt)
       wcnt /= wcnt.sum()
       w = np.zeros(2*n-1); w[idx]=wcnt
       g = weighted_gradient_counts(w,h,n)*(2.0/n)
       d = -g
       d = d - d.mean()
       v = 0.7*v + d
       d = v
       mn = np.max(np.abs(d))
       if mn < 1e-12: stall +=1; continue
       d = d/mn
       improved=False
       for step in [0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001]:
          y = project_feasible(h + step*d, n)
          cc = correlation_counts_fft(y) # or np.correlate for exact? Use FFT; final exact later
          val = float(cc.max())*(2.0/n)
          if val < best_count - 1e-12:
             h=y; c=cc; best_count=val; best_h=h.copy(); improved=True; v=0.0; break
       if improved: stall=0
       else:
          stall +=1
          if stall>20: break
         # maybe reduce alpha_w? or random
    h,best= best_h,best_count
    # recompute c? not needed
    return h,best
```
This uses FFT counts; candidate may have floating; okay. It projects each step; 8*80=640 projections/FFT. Good. But `project_feasible` bisection can be 50* n each; n=250 ->2M per 640? 12.5k*640=8M, fine.
Maybe `project_feasible` clipping y+step*d with step 0.2 changes small, tau near 0? Bisection over y range maybe ~1; 40 iters. Good.
We can use direct `np.correlate` for val for exact, but FFT faster. Use FFT.

However, subgradient direction from top active may be very random. It might improve little. But no harm if time.

Let's test update formula with a simple example mentally. n=2, a=[a0,a1], b=[b0,b1], c=conv(a,b_rev=[b1,b0]) = [a0b1, a0b0+a1b1, a1b0]. Now move i=0 +d, j=1 -d. Old brev=[b1,b0]. update:
point i=0 +d: c[0:2] += d*brev -> c0 +=d b1, c1 +=d b0.
s=n-1-0=1: c[1:3] -= d*a -> c1 -=d a0, c2 -=d a1.
point j=1 -d (val=-d): c[1:3] += -d*brev -> c1 -=d b1, c2 -=d b0.
s=0: c[0:2] -= val*a? Formula c[s:s+n] -= val*a, val=-d -> c0 +=d a0, c1 +=d a1.
quadratic: c[n-1=1] -=2d^2; p1=i+n-1-j=0+1-1=0 -> c0 +=d^2; p2=j+n-1-i=1+1-0=2 -> c2 +=d^2.
Sum linear changes:
c0: d b1 + d a0 + d^2.
New c0 for a0+d, a1-d: (a0+d)b1_new? b0,b1 old? b for a1-d = 1-a1+d = old b1 + d. c0 = new a0 * new b1 = (a0+d)(b1+d)=a0b1 + d(b1+a0)+d^2. Matches.
c1: d b0 - d a0 - d b1 + d a1 -2d^2. New c1 = (a0+d)b0_new? Wait c1 = new a0 * b0_new + new a1 * b1_new. b0 changes? a0 changes, so b0_new=b0-d; b1_new=b1+d. New a1=a1-d. Product = (a0+d)(b0-d)+(a1-d)(b1+d)= a0b0-a0d+a0d? Let's expand: a0b0 - a0d + d b0 - d^2 + a1b1 + a1d - d b1 - d^2 = a0b0+a1b1 + d(b0 - a0 + a1 - b1) -2d^2. Matches.
c2: -d a1 -d b0 + d^2. New c2 = new a1 * b0_new = (a1-d)(b0-d)=a1b0 -a1d -b0d +d^2. Matches. Good.

Feasibility: c counts can be slightly negative? no.

Good. Max reduction if active weights. Good.

Maybe simpler: A loop while time, with adaptive delta. Recompute gradient occasionally. For each trial, choose pair: mostly deterministic low/high, some random. Try one or two deltas. Accept if improve. If no improvement for `stall` trials, multiply current `delta` by 0.5. If delta <1e-4 or stall >50000 break. This avoids epoch complexity. Pseudo:
```
def fast_pair_polish(...):
  h,best...
  delta = base_delta
  stall = 0
  total_trials=0
  last_g = -1
  order=None; low_high=None
  while time < deadline and delta > 1e-4:
    total_trials +=1
    if total_trials % 200 ==0:
       # recompute gradient, order
       ...
       low=order[:L]; high=order[-L:][::-1]
       last_g=total_trials
    if total_trials % 5000 ==0 and stall>2000:
       delta *=0.5; stall=0
       if delta <1e-4: break
    if random.random() < 0.2 or order is None:
       i=rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n)
    else:
       # choose random from low/high with possible variation
       i=low[rng.randint(len(low))]; j=high[rng.randint(len(high))]
    if i==j: continue
    # sign from g if available else +
    if g is not None:
       sign = 1 if g[i] <= g[j] else -1
    else: sign = 1
    # try dvals: delta, 0.5 delta if delta>0.003
    dvals = [sign*delta]
    if delta > 0.003: dvals.append(0.5*sign*delta)
    for dv in dvals:
       applied=apply...
       if applied is None: continue
       new_count=c.max()
       # exact recheck drift occasionally
       if total_trials % 2000 ==0:
          c_exact=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); current_count=max
          if current_count < best_count: update; best_count=current
          elif current_count > best_count + 1e-7:
             h=best_h.copy(); c=np.correlate(h,1-h); brev=...; current_count=best_count
          c = c_exact? Wait after applying and maybe before rollback? We need exact c for current state. If recompute after move, c should be exact new. Set c=c_exact; brev=(1-h)[::-1]; current_count. Then compare.
          continue? We should decide accept based on exact current_count, not new_count.
       if new_count < current_count - tol:
          current_count = new_count
          if current_count < best_count: best_count=current; best_h=h.copy()
          stall=0
          break # accept and exit dvals
       else:
          revert...
    else:
       stall +=1
       if stall > 3000:
          delta *= 0.5; stall=0
          if delta <1e-4: break
    # if total_trials % 2000 recompute and if no improve? 
  return best_h,best_bound

`active_gradient_counts(p_indices, p_values, x,n)`:
```
g = np.zeros(n)
b = 1.0 - x
for p,w in zip(p_indices,p_values):
    d = p - (n-1)
    # first: add w * b[m-d] for m range
    start = d if d > 0 else 0
    end = n if d < 0 else n + d
    # if start < end:
    if 0 <= d <= n-1? Let's test d=1: m=1..n-1? m-d 0..n-2. start=1,end=n? n+d= n+1 min n? end=n. slice m[1:n], e[0:n-1]. Good. d=-1: m-d=m+1 valid m=0..n-2. start=0,end=n-1? n+d = n-1. e=start-d=1 to end-d= n-1? b[1:n]. But formula first term b[m-d] with d=-1: b[m+1], yes m 0..n-2. Good.
    if start < end:
       e_start = start - d
       e_end = end - d
       g[start:end] += w * b[e_start:e_end]
    # second subtract w*a[m+d]
    # m range: m+d in 0..n-1 => m in [-d, n-1-d] ∩ [0,n-1]
    start2 = -d if d < 0 else 0
    end2 = n if d > 0 else n - d  # if d positive, n-d; if d negative, n + |d| min n? Let's use min(n, n-d)
    end2 = min(n, n - d)
    if start2 < end2:
       e_start = start2 + d
       e_end = end2 + d
       g[start2:end2] -= w * a[e_start:e_end]
return g
```

`_fix_sum(x,n)`:
```
def _fix_sum(x, n):
    S = 0.5 * n
    diff = S - float(x.sum())
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    if abs(diff) < 1e-12:
        return x
    if diff > 0.0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - x))
        add = min(diff, 1.0 - x[idx])
        if add > 0.0:
            x[idx] += add
            diff -= add
        if diff > 1e-14:
            idxs = np.argsort(1.0 - x)[::-1]
            for k in idxs:
                if diff <= 1e-14: break
                add = min(diff, 1.0 - x[k])
                if add > 0:
                    x[k] += add; diff -= add
    else:
        idx = int(np.argmax(x))
        sub = min(-diff, x[idx])
        if sub > 0.0:
            x[idx] -= sub; diff += sub
        if diff < -1e-14:
            idxs = np.argsort(x)
            for k in idxs:
                if diff >= -1e-14: break
                sub = min(-diff, x[k])
                if sub > 0:
                    x[k] -= sub; diff += sub
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    return x
```
If diff remains (extreme infeasible), that's okay? Projection should fix.

`project_feasible` uses `y=np.asarray(y,float)`. If length !=n? Maybe slice/pad? Not needed. But if initial_h_values maybe list length; if n mismatch due? We call with correct n. If y has nan, replace? `y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)`.
Bisection range: Suppose y min=0,max=0; lo=-1,hi=1? Actually lo=y.min()-1 = -1, hi=y.max()+1 =1? For tau=-1, x=clip(y+1,0,1)=1 if y=0 -> sum n. tau=1 -> x=0. Good. Use 45 iterations. If y values large, bounds fine.

lo = float(y.min()) - 1.0 - 1e-12
hi = float(y.max()) + 1.0? To ensure x=0 at hi. If y max=1, hi=2; tau=2 x=0. ok.
for 45:
 tau=...
 s=np.clip(y-tau,0,1).sum()
 if s > S: lo=tau else hi=tau

```
At tau=lo maybe sum >=S, hi <=. Good.

`fft_convolve`:
```
def fft_convolve(a,b):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); b=np.asarray(b,float)
    n=a.size; m=b.size
    if n==0 or m==0: return np.array([],float)
    L=1
    need=n+m-1
    while L < need: L <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L); fb=np.fft.rfft(b,L)
    out=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,L)
    return out[:need]
```
Maybe for small need <64 use direct `np.convolve` faster/less error. Add:
```
if need <= 128: return np.convolve(a,b)
```
But np.convolve may be direct. For n=250 need 499 >128. For small coarse. Good.

`smooth_objective`:
```
def smooth_objective(x,n,alpha):
    x=np.asarray(x,float)
    if x.shape[0] != n:
       # maybe flatten
       x=x.reshape(-1)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return 1e100
    dx=2.0/n
    c=correlation_counts_fft(x)*dx
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)): return 1e100
    m=c.max()
    mA=alpha*(c-m)
    shift=mA.max()
    s=np.sum(np.exp(mA-shift))
    return float(m + (shift + np.log(s))/alpha)
```
If alpha large and s maybe 0? no.

`smooth_gradient`:
```
def smooth_gradient(x,n,alpha):
   x=np.asarray(x,float)
   if x.shape[0] != n: x=x.reshape(-1)
   grad=np.zeros(n,float)
   if not finite: return grad
   dx=2/n
   c=correlation_counts_fft(x)*dx
   m=c.max(); mA=alpha*(c-m); shift=mA.max(); e=np.exp(mA-shift); p=e/e.sum()
   g=dense_gradient_counts(p,x,n)*dx
   return g
```
`dense_gradient_counts(p,x,n)`:
```
def dense_gradient_counts(p,x,n):
   p=np.asarray(p,float)
   x=np.asarray(x,float)
   b=1.0-x
   g1=fft_convolve(b,p)[n-1:2*n-1]
   g2=fft_convolve(x[::-1], p)[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
   return g1-g2
```
Check slices: `2*n-1` length; `n-1:2*n-1` gives indices n-1..2n-2 length n. `g2` reversed. Good.

`active_gradient_counts(idx, values, x,n)`: `idx` int array, values float array same len. Could loop. Use b precompute.
Implementation with careful slices:
```
def active_gradient_counts(idx, vals, x, n):
    g=np.zeros(n,float)
    if idx.size ==0: return g
    a=x; b=1-a
    np1=n-1
    for t in range(idx.size):
       p=int(idx[t]); w=float(vals[t])
       if w == 0.0: continue
       d=p-np1
       # first
       if 0 <= p? no
       start = d if d > 0 else 0
       end = n if d < 0 else n + d  # because if d positive, n+d < n? Wait if d positive e.g. 1, end = n+1? But min with n needed.
```
Let's compute formula first again: m range e=m-d, e=0..n-1. e + d in 0..n. start = max(0, -d?) No, m=e+d. Minimum m = max(0, d) when e= max(0,-d)? But e must be >=0. If d positive: e ranges 0..n-1-d so m=d..n-1. start=d, end=n. If d negative: e ranges -d..n-1 so m=0..n-1+d (n-|d|). start=0, end=n+d (since d negative). So:
```
if d >=0:
  start=d; end=n
else:
  start=0; end=n+d
```
Wait for d=-1, end=n-1, good. For d=0 start0,end n. Good.
But if d > n-1? p range 0..2n-2 so d in [-(n-1),n-1]; end nonnegative.
```
if start < end:
  e_start = start - d
  e_end = end - d
  g[start:end] += w * b[e_start:e_end]
```
Check d=1: e_start=0, e_end=n-1, b[0:n-1], m[1:n]. Good. d=-1: e_start=1, e_end=n, b[1:n], m[0:n-1]. Good.
Second: m+d=e (0..n-1). e=m+d. If d >=0: m=0..n-1-d -> start0,end=n-d, e_start=d, e_end=n. If d<0: m=-d..n-1 -> start=-d,end=n, e_start=0,e_end=n+d. So:
```
if d >=0:
  start2=0; end2=n-d
else:
  start2=-d; end2=n
if start2<end2:
  e_start=start2+d
  e_end=end2+d
  g[start2:end2] -= w * a[e_start:e_end]
```
Good.
This direct gradient of counts. Multiply by dx outside.

`top_lag_weights(c,k,alpha=2000)`:
```
def top_lag_weights(c,k,alpha):
   idx=top_lag_indices(c,k)
   if idx.size==0: return idx, np.array([])
   cmax=float(c.max())
   v=c[idx]-cmax
   # temperature in counts: alpha maybe 10000? v negative. If min v < -1, exp underflow but okay.
   e=np.exp(v*alpha)
   s=e.sum()
   if s <= 1e-300 or not finite: w=np.ones(k)/k
   else: w=e/s
   return idx,w
```
Use K maybe 8. alpha large makes uniform among exact ties? If c differences small, okay. For pair sorting, weights not matter for g; but if one max, gradient from max. Maybe top K uniform better to avoid overemphasis if numerical. Maybe use uniform over top K for pair. But active weights can be uniform top 8. Simpler. For subgradient, use softmax or uniform? Use uniform to stabilize. Could make `top_lag_weights_uniform`. In gradient, g direction from average of top lags. Let's use uniform top K. In situations where max unique, top includes next close lags; smoothing helps. For SLSQP LSE dense handles.

`subgradient_polish`:
```
def subgradient_polish(h,n,deadline,rng,max_iters=60):
    h=project_feasible(h.astype(float,copy=True),n)
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
    best_count=float(c.max()); best_c=best_count*2/n; best_h=h.copy()
    v=np.zeros(n)
    stall=0
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.time() > deadline: break
       idx=top_lag_indices(c,8)
       w=np.ones(idx.size)/idx.size
       g=active_gradient_counts(idx,w,h,n)*(2.0/n)
       d=-g
       d-=d.mean()
       v=0.7*v+d
       d=v
       m=np.max(np.abs(d))
       if m < 1e-14: stall+=1; continue
       d=d/m
       improved=False
       for step in [0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001]:
           y=project_feasible(h+step*d,n)
           cc=correlation_counts_fft(y)
           val=float(cc.max())*(2.0/n)
           if val < best_c - 1e-12:
               h=y; c=cc; best_c=val; best_count=best_c*(n/2.0); best_h=h.copy(); improved=True; v=0.0; break
       if improved: stall=0
       else:
          stall+=1
          if stall>15: break
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')? Not needed; best_h maybe.
    # Return best_h and exact bound
    hc=project_feasible(best_h,n)
    bc=evaluate_exact(hc)
    return hc,bc
```
Note if `correlation_counts_fft` has small error, val might be slightly off; final exact. Accept if val < best_c - 1e-12. May miss tiny. Fine. Since subgradient only heuristic. It uses `best_c` as current best, so only global improvements; current state always best. Good.
But if a step doesn't immediately improve but enables later, strict may stall. Fine.

But `project_feasible` in subgrad with h+step*d where d mean zero, sum approx S; projection. 8 steps each iter. If no improve, no state change. Good.

SLSQP smooth:
Potential issue: `minimize` can be very slow due evaluating dense LSE for n=500. We can limit. Use `maxiter` small. Also SLSQP may not respect feasibility due equality. After each alpha, project and exact.
```
def slsqp_smooth(h,n,alpha,maxiter):
   bounds=[(0.0,1.0) for _ in range(n)]
   cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':_sum_eq,'jac':_sum_eq_jac,'args':(n,)}]
   try:
      res=minimize(smooth_objective,h,args=(n,alpha),method='SLSQP',jac=smooth_gradient,bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12,'disp':False})
      x=project_feasible(np.asarray(res.x,float),n)
   except Exception:
      x=project_feasible(h,n)
   return x
```
Potential memory: args tuple passed. SLSQP may call jac with x and args. Good.
`_sum_eq_jac` returns ones. Shape (n,). Good.

`fast_pair_polish` implementation in detail. Let's write robust but not overly complex.
```
def fast_pair_polish(h,n,deadline,rng,base_delta=0.02,target_tries=None):
    h = project_feasible(np.array(h,float,copy=True),n)
    c = np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full').copy()
    brev = (1.0-h)[::-1].copy()
    best_count = float(c.max())
    best_c = best_count * (2.0/n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    delta = base_delta
    stall = 0
    total = 0
    g = None; low=None; high=None
    pair_list = None; cursor=0; epoch_acc=0; epoch_len=0
    improve_tol = 1e-8
    min_delta=1e-5
    while time.time() < deadline - 1:
        total += 1
        # periodic recompute
        if total % 1000 == 0:
            c_exact = np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
            actual = float(c_exact.max())
            if actual < best_count - improve_tol:
                best_count=actual; best_c=actual*2/n; best_h=h.copy()
            elif actual > best_count + 1e-6:
                h=best_h.copy()
            c = np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full').copy()
            brev = (1.0-h)[::-1].copy()
            # If c max not best but close, okay. If actual best? best_count unchanged.
            # If actual < best_count already updated.
            # Need if actual > best_count and we didn't rollback (within 1e-6), c max > best; okay.
            # Maybe reset g stale
            g=None
        if g is None:
            idx = top_lag_indices(c, 10)
            w = np.ones(idx.size, dtype=float)/max(1,idx.size)
            g = active_gradient_counts(idx, w, h, n)  # counts derivative, no dx? For sorting no dx matters
            order = np.argsort(g)
            L = min(40, n//2)
            low = order[:L]
            high = order[-L:][::-1]
            # build pairs if none
        if pair_list is None or cursor >= len(pair_list):
            plist=[]
            if low is not None:
                for i in low:
                    for j in high:
                        if i != j:
                            plist.append((int(i), int(j)))
                if len(plist) > 400:
                    rng.shuffle(plist)
                    plist = plist[:400]  # limit? maybe don't limit
            if not plist:
                for _ in range(100):
                    i=rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n)
                    if i!=j: plist.append((i,j))
            pair_list=plist
            cursor=0
            epoch_acc=0
        if pair_list is not None and cursor < len(pair_list) and (g is None or rng.random() < 0.85):
            i,j = pair_list[cursor]
        else:
            i = int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
            if i == j:
                j = (j+1) % n
        cursor += 1
        # Determine sign
        if g is not None:
            if g[i] <= g[j]: delta_sign=1.0
            else: delta_sign=-1.0
            # if gradient difference very small, random sign
            if abs(g[i]-g[j]) < 1e-8:
                delta_sign = 1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1.0
        else:
            delta_sign = 1.0 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1.0
        tried=False
        dvals = [delta_sign * delta]
        if delta > 0.004:
            dvals.append(0.5 * delta_sign * delta)
        for dv in dvals:
            applied = apply_pair_move(h,brev,c,i,j,dv)
            if applied is None:
                continue
            new_count = float(c.max())
            if new_count < best_count - improve_tol:
                best_count = new_count
                best_c = new_count * (2.0/n)
                best_h = h.copy()
                epoch_acc += 1
                stall = 0
                tried = True
                # if many accepts, maybe force new pairs
                if epoch_acc >= 8:
                    pair_list = None
                break
            else:
                revert_pair_move(h,brev,c,i,j,applied)
                tried = True
        # if no feasible dv, just count stall
        if cursor >= len(pair_list) or pair_list is None:
            if epoch_acc == 0:
                delta *= 0.5
                if delta < min_delta:
                    break
            pair_list = None
            # If no improvement in epoch, maybe if delta decreased too many? stall=0
            stall = 0
        else:
            if tried and not (best_count < ...?) We need know if improvement in this trial. We can set `improved_trial` bool.
```
Need track `improved_trial` inside loop. Add before dvals: `improved_trial=False`; set true when accept. After dvals:

if improved_trial: stall=0
else:
   stall +=1
   if stall > 3000:
      delta *=0.5; stall=0; if delta<min_delta: break
```

When epoch_acc ==0 and new pair_list, reduce delta. But if epoch built small list and no improve because pair_list exhausted; good.

# Also maybe current h if c exact lower?
c_cur = np.correlate(h,1-h,'full').max()*2/n

def optimize_resolution(h, n, deadline, rng):
    h = project_feasible(np.array(h,float,copy=True),n)
    best_c = evaluate_exact(h); best_h=h.copy()
    # SLSQP first? or subgradient? Maybe start with subgradient (fast) then SLSQP then pair.
    if n <= 800 and time.time() < deadline - 30:
       sg = min(45 if n<=400 else 20, deadline - 30)
       h_s, c_s = subgradient_polish(h,n,time.time()+sg,rng)
       if c_s < best_c: best_h, best_c = h_s, c_s
    # SLSQP
    if n <= 700 and time.time() < deadline - 40:
       if n <= 300:
          alphas=[50,300,1500,8000,40000]
          maxiters=[120,120,100,80,60]
       elif n <= 500:
          alphas=[100,800,4000]
          maxiters=[80,70,50]
       else:
          alphas=[200,1500]
          maxiters=[50,40]
       for a,mx in zip(alphas,maxiters):
          if time.time() > deadline - 30: break
          ht = slsqp_smooth(best_h,n,a,mx) # restart from best each alpha
          c=evaluate_exact(ht)
          if c < best_c - 1e-14:
              best_h=ht.copy(); best_c=c
    # final pair
    if time.time() < deadline - 5:
       # choose pair deadline and reserve anneal? We'll let use most, then if best improved maybe anneal separately in run.
       hp, cp = fast_pair_polish(best_h,n,deadline - 5,rng,base_delta=0.02)
       if cp < best_c: best_h,best_c=hp,cp
    return best_h,best_c
```

```

    h=project...

       # choose alpha schedule
       ...
       for ...:
          if time.time() > deadline - 20: break
          # Reserve for pair: if remaining < needed, break. SLSQP could exceed; use maxiter small.
          ht=slsqp_smooth(best_h,n,a,mx)
          c=evaluate_exact(ht)
          if c < best_c - 1e-13:
             best_h=ht.copy(); best_c=c
    # pair if time > 10
    if time.time() < deadline - 2:
       hp,cp=fast_pair_polish(best_h,n,deadline-2,rng,base_delta=0.02)
       if cp < best_c: best_h,best_c=hp,cp
    return best_h,best_c
```

```
import time
import math
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def _fft_len(N):
    L=1
    while L < N:
        L <<= 1
    return L

def fft_convolve(a,b):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    b=np.asarray(b,float)
    need=a.size+b.size-1
    if a.size ==0 or b.size ==0: return np.zeros(0)
    if need <= 128:
        return np.convolve(a,b)
    L=_fft_len(need)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    B=np.fft.rfft(b,L)
    out=np.fft.irfft(A*B,L)
    return out[:need]
...
```

`project_feasible`:
```
def project_feasible(y,n):
    y=np.asarray(y,float).reshape(-1)
    if y.size != n:
       tmp=np.zeros(n)
       m=min(n,y.size); tmp[:m]=y[:m]
       y=tmp
    y=np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    S=0.5*n
    lo=float(y.min()) - 1.0 - 1e-9
    hi=float(y.max()) + 1.0 + 1e-9
    for _ in range(50):
       tau=0.5*(lo+hi)
       s=np.clip(y-tau,0.0,1.0).sum()
       if s > S:
          lo=tau
       else:
          hi=tau
    x=np.clip(y-0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,1.0)
    return _fix_sum(x,n)
```
If n=0? not.

`_fix_sum`: Need avoid `np.argsort` on large each call. But projection called many; fixing sum after bisection diff small, can just one adjust. Use one adjust and if remains, small distribute but not argsort every time? Could only argsort if diff large. But after bisection diff tiny, one adjust enough. Implement one adjust to preserve speed:
```
def _fix_sum(x,n):
  S=0.5*n; diff=S-x.sum(); x=np.clip(x,0,1)
  if abs(diff) < 1e-14: return x
  if diff>0:
    idx=int(np.argmax(1-x)); add=min(diff,1-x[idx]); x[idx]+=add; diff-=add
  else:
    idx=int(np.argmax(x)); sub=min(-diff,x[idx]); x[idx]-=sub; diff+=sub
  # if still diff, distribute to top 32 no full argsort? Use argmax repeatedly maybe.
  if abs(diff)>1e-14:
     if diff>0:
        for _ in range(min(64,n)):
           idx=int(np.argmax(1-x)); add=min(diff,1-x[idx]); if add<=1e-16: break; x[idx]+=add; diff-=add; if diff<=1e-14 break
     else:
        for _ in range(min(64,n)):
           idx=int(np.argmin(x)); sub=min(-diff,x[idx]); if sub<=1e-16: break; x[idx]-=sub; diff+=sub; if diff>=-1e-14 break
  return np.clip(x,0,1)
```
This avoids argsort. 64* projection rare; if diff small one. Good.

`top_lag_indices` if k<=0 return empty array int.
```
def top_lag_indices(c,k):
    m=c.size
    if k <=0 or m<=0: return np.empty(0,int)
    if k >= m: return np.arange(m,int)
    return np.argpartition(c, m-k)[-k:]
```

`active_gradient_counts`: Use local vars, for speed.
```
def active_gradient_counts(idx,vals,x,n):
    g=np.zeros(n,float)
    a=x; b=1.0-a; np1=n-1; L=idx.size
    for t in range(L):
       p=int(idx[t]); w=float(vals[t])
       if w == 0.0: continue
       d=p-np1
       if d >=0:
          start=d; end=n
          if start<end:
             g[start:end] += w*b[:end-start]
          start2=0; end2=n-d
          if start2<end2:
             g[start2:end2] -= w*a[d:end2+d]
       else:
          start=0; end=n+d
          if start<end:
             estart=-d
             g[start:end] += w*b[ecast? estart:eastype]
```
Let's keep general:
```
if d >=0:
  s1=d; e1=n
  if s1<e1:
    g[s1:e1] += w * b[:e1-s1]
  s2=0; e2=n-d
  if s2<e2:
    g[s2:e2] -= w * a[d:e2+d]
else:
  s1=0; e1=n+d
  if s1<e1:
    g[s1:e1] += w * b[-d:]
  s2=-d; e2=n
  if s2<e2:

```

def _update_pair_counts(a,brev,c,i,j,delta):
    n=a.shape[0]
    c[i:i+n] += delta * brev
    s=n-1-i
    c[s:s+n] -= delta * a
    v=-delta
    c[j:j+n] += v * brev
    s=n-1-j
    c[s:s+n] -= v * a
    quad = delta*delta
    c[n-1] -= 2.0*quad
    p = i + n - 1 - j
    if p != n-1: c[p] += quad
    p = j + n - 1 - i
    if p != n-1: c[p] += quad
```

def revert_pair_move(h,brev,c,i,j,delta):
    if delta ==0: return
    _update_pair_counts(h,brev,c,i,j,-delta)
    h[i]-=delta; h[j]+=delta

`fast_pair_polish` final code more complex. Let's write pseudo fully:
```
def fast_pair_polish(h,n,deadline,rng,base_delta=0.02):
    if n < 3:
       return h, evaluate_exact(h)
    h=project_feasible(np.array(h,float,copy=True),n)
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full').copy()
    brev=(1.0-h)[::-1].copy()
    best_count=float(c.max()); best_c=best_count*(2.0/n); best_h=h.copy()
    delta=base_delta; stall=0; total=0
    g=None; low=None; high=None; pair_list=None; cursor=0; epoch_acc=0
    improve_tol=1e-8
    min_delta=1e-5
    while time.time() < deadline - 1.0:
        total += 1
        if total % 1000 == 0:
            c_exact = np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
            actual=float(np.max(c_exact))
            if actual < best_count - improve_tol:
                best_count=actual; best_c=actual*(2.0/n); best_h=h.copy()
            if actual > best_count + 1e-6:
                h=best_h.copy()
            c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full').copy()
            brev=(1.0-h)[::-1].copy()
            g=None
        if g is None:
            idx=top_lag_indices(c,10)
            if idx.size >0:
                w=np.ones(idx.size, dtype=float)/float(idx.size)
                g=active_gradient_counts(idx,w,h,n)
                order=np.argsort(g)
                L=min(40, n//2)
                low=order[:L]
                high=order[-L:][::-1]
            else:
                low=np.empty(0,int); high=np.empty(0,int)
        if pair_list is None or cursor >= len(pair_list):
            plist=[]
            if low is not None and low.size>0 and high is not None and high.size>0:
                for ii in low:
                    i=int(ii)
                    for jj in high:
                        j=int(jj)
                        if i != j:
                            plist.append((i,j))
                if len(plist) > 400:
                    rng.shuffle(plist)
                    plist=plist[:400]
                else:
                    rng.shuffle(plist)
            if not plist:
                for _ in range(50):
                    i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
                    if i != j: plist.append((i,j))
            pair_list=plist
            cursor=0
            epoch_acc=0
        if pair_list is not None and cursor < len(pair_list) and rng.random() < 0.9:
            i,j=pair_list[cursor]
        else:
            i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
            if i==j:
                j=(j+1)%n
        cursor += 1
        if g is not None and low is not None:
            if g[i] <= g[j]:
                dsign=1.0
            else:
                dsign=-1.0
            if abs(g[i]-g[j]) < 1e-10:
                dsign=1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1.0
        else:
            dsign=1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1.0
        improved_trial=False
        any_feas=False
        dvals=[dsign*delta]
        if delta > 0.004:
            dvals.append(0.5*dsign*delta)
        for dv in dvals:
            applied=apply_pair_move(h,brev,c,i,j,dv)
            if applied is None:
                continue
            any_feas=True
            new_count=float(np.max(c))
            if new_count < best_count - improve_tol:
                best_count=new_count
                best_c=new_count*(2.0/n)
                best_h=h.copy()
                improved_trial=True
                epoch_acc += 1
                if epoch_acc >= 8:
                    pair_list=None
                    g=None
                break
            else:
                revert_pair_move(h,brev,c,i,j,applied)
        if not any_feas:
            # try opposite sign quickly
            for dv in [-dvals[0], -dvals[1] if len(dvals)>1 else 0.5?]:
             ...
```

`anneal_pair_polish` code:
```
def anneal_pair_polish(h,n,deadline,rng):
    if n<3: return h, evaluate_exact(h)
    h=project_feasible(np.array(h,float,copy=True),n)
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full').copy(); brev=...
    best_count=...; best_c=...; best_h=h.copy(); current_count=best_count
    T0c=0.0005; T1c=1e-6
    start=time.time(); total_time=max(1, deadline-start)
    dec_enc=200000
    it=0
    while time.time() < deadline - 1:
       it +=1
       frac=min(1.0, it/dec_enc)
       Tc=T0c*(T1c/T0c)**frac
       Tcount=Tc*(n/2.0)
       i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
       if i==j: continue
       mag=(0.002 + 0.05*(1.0-frac))*(0.5+rng.random())
       delta=(mag if rng.random()<0.5 else -mag)
       applied=apply_pair_move(...)
       if applied is None: continue
       new_count=float(np.max(c))
       diff=new_count-current_count
       accept=False
       if diff < 0.0:
          accept=True
       elif Tcount > 0 and diff < 3*Tcount:
          u=rng.random()
          if u >0 and math.log(u) < -diff/Tcount:
             accept=True
       if accept:
          current_count=new_count
          if new_count < best_count:
             best_count=new_count; best_c=...; best_h=h.copy()
       else:
          revert_pair_move(...)
       if it % 2000 ==0:
          c_exact=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
          actual=float(np.max(c_exact))
          if actual < best_count:
             best_count=actual; best_c=...; best_h=h.copy()
          if actual > best_count + 0.5:
             h=best_h.copy()
             c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full').copy(); brev=...; current_count=best_count
          else:
             c=c_exact.copy(); brev=(1-h)[::-1].copy(); current_count=actual
    # final
    h_best=project_feasible(best_h,n); c_best=evaluate_exact(h_best)
    return h_best,c_best
```
Potential: `current_count` after rollback if actual > best+0.5; if actual within, current_count=actual (may be > best). Good.
If accepted non-improving, c approximate; current_count new_count approximate. Periodic recompute corrects.

`optimize_resolution` final:
```
def optimize_resolution(h,n,deadline,rng):
    h=project_feasible(np.array(h,float,copy=True),n)
    best_c=evaluate_exact(h); best_h=h.copy()
    if n < 3: return best_h,best_c
    if n <= 350 and time.time() < deadline - 30:
       alloc=min(25, deadline-time.time()-10)
       if alloc > 8:
          hs,cs=subgradient_polish(h,n,time.time()+alloc,rng,max_iters=25)
          if cs < best_c - 1e-14: best_h,best_c=hs,cs
    if n <= 700 and time.time() < deadline - 25:
       rem=deadline-time.time()
       if n <= 350:
          sched=[(200,70),(1500,60),(10000,40)]
       elif n <= 600:
          sched=[(500,40),(3000,30)]
       else:
          sched=[(1000,25)]
       for alpha,mx in sched:
          if time.time() > deadline - 15: break
          if rem < 30: break
          ht=slsqp_smooth(best_h,n,alpha,mx)
          c=evaluate_exact(ht)
          if c < best_c - 1e-13:
             best_h=ht.copy(); best_c=c
    if time.time() < deadline - 3:
       hp,cp=fast_pair_polish(best_h,n,deadline-3,rng,base_delta=0.02)
       if cp < best_c - 1e-14:
          best_h,best_c=hp,cp
    return best_h,best_c
```
Sched alpha 10000 maybe high; if no time. Good.

In `run`, after first optimize, if c1 improved. But if first optimize returns same as best, no change. Then refine.

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    if budget_s <= 30: deadline=t0+budget_s-2 else deadline=t0+max(30,budget_s)-15
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    best_h=None; best_c=inf
    try:
       par=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).reshape(-1)
       n=len(par)
       if n > 0:
          if par.min() >= -1e-8 and par.max() <= 1+1e-8 and abs(par.sum()-0.5*n) < 1e-6:
             h0=np.clip(par,0.0,1.0)
             h0=_fix_sum(h0,n)
          else:
             h0=project_feasible(par,n)
          best_h=h0.copy()
          best_c=evaluate_exact(h0)
    except Exception:
       n=250
       best_h=initial_construction(n)
       best_c=evaluate_exact(best_h)
    if best_c == inf or not math.isfinite(best_c):
       best_h=initial_construction(250); best_c=evaluate_exact(best_h)
    # first optimize
    rem=deadline-time.time()
    if rem > 60:
       first_alloc=max(30, min(480, rem*0.55))
       if first_alloc > 120:
          first_dead=time.time()+first_alloc
          if first_dead > deadline - 80: first_dead=deadline-80
          if first_dead > time.time()+20:
             h1,c1=optimize_resolution(best_h,len(best_h),first_dead,rng)
             if c1 < best_c: best_h,best_c=h1,c1
    # refine
    rem=deadline-time.time()
    nbest=len(best_h)
    if rem > 120 and nbest <= 350:
       ntry=500
       alloc=min(320, rem-80)
       if alloc > 70 and ntry != nbest:
          x_old=(np.arange(nbest)+0.5)*(2.0/nbest)
          x_new=(np.arange(ntry)+0.5)*(2.0/ntry)
          h_try=np.interp(x_new,x_old,best_h)
          h_try=project_feasible(h_try,ntry)
          h2,c2=optimize_resolution(h_try,ntry,time.time()+alloc,rng)
          if c2 < best_c:
             best_h,best_c=h2,c2
    # final polish/anneal
    rem=deadline-time.time()
    n=len(best_h)
    if rem > 25:
       pair_alloc=max(10, min(120, rem*0.5))
       pair_dead=time.time()+pair_alloc
       if pair_dead > deadline-15: pair_dead=deadline-15
       if pair_dead > time.time()+5:
          hf,cf=fast_pair_polish(best_h,n,pair_dead,rng,base_delta=0.006)
          if cf < best_c: best_h,best_c=hf,cf
       rem=deadline-time.time()
       if rem > 45:
          ha,ca=anneal_pair_polish(best_h,n,deadline-10,rng)
          if ca < best_c: best_h,best_c=ha,ca
    # final
    h=project_feasible(best_h,n)
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    h=_fix_sum(h,n)
    c=evaluate_exact(h)
    # if final worse than best_c, try best_h raw
    if c > best_c + 1e-11:
       h2=project_feasible(best_h,n); c2=evaluate_exact(h2)
       if c2 < c: h,c=h2,c2
    if not math.isfinite(c) or c < 0:
       h=np.full(n,0.5); c=evaluate_exact(h)
    return h,float(c),int(n)
```

```python
import time
import math
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def _fft_len(N):
    L = 1
    while L < N:
        L <<= 1
    return L


def fft_convolve(a, b):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=float)
    need = a.size + b.size - 1
    if a.size == 0 or b.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=float)
    if need <= 128:
        return np.convolve(a, b)
    L = _fft_len(need)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    B = np.fft.rfft(b, L)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * B, L)
    return out[:need]


def correlation_counts_fft(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    return fft_convolve(h, (1.0 - h)[::-1])


def _fix_sum(x, n):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.asarray(x, dtype=float).reshape(0)
    x = np.clip(np.asarray(x, dtype=float).reshape(n), 0.0, 1.0)
    S = 0.5 * n
    diff = S - float(x.sum())
    if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
        return x
    if diff > 0.0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - x))
        add = min(diff, max(0.0, 1.0 - x[idx]))
        if add > 0.0:
            x[idx] += add
            diff -= add
    else:
        idx = int(np.argmax(x))
        sub = min(-diff, max(0.0, x[idx]))
        if sub > 0.0:
            x[idx] -= sub
            diff += sub
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        if diff > 0.0:
            for _ in range(min(96, n)):
                idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - x))
                add = min(diff, max(0.0, 1.0 - x[idx]))
                if add <= 1e-15:
                    break
                x[idx] += add
                diff -= add
                if abs(diff) <= 1e-14:
                    break
        else:
            for _ in range(min(96, n)):
                idx = int(np.argmin(x))
                sub = min(-diff, max(0.0, x[idx]))
                if sub <= 1e-15:
                    break
                x[idx] -= sub
                diff += sub
                if abs(diff) <= 1e-14:
                    break
    return np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)


def project_feasible(y, n):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.asarray(y, dtype=float).reshape(0)
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    if y.size != n:
        tmp = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        m = min(n, y.size)
        if m > 0:
            tmp[:m] = y[:m]
        y = tmp
    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    S = 0.5 * n
    lo = float(y.min()) - 1.0 - 1e-9
    hi = float(y.max()) + 1.0 + 1e-9
    for _ in range(50):
        tau = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.clip(y - tau, 0.0, 1.0).sum())
        if s > S:
            lo = tau
        else:
            hi = tau
    x = np.clip(y - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    return _fix_sum(x, n)


def evaluate_exact(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
        return float('inf')
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / n))


def top_lag_indices(c, k):
    m = c.size
    if k <= 0 or m <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=int)
    if k >= m:
        return np.arange(m, dtype=int)
    return np.argpartition(c, m - k)[-k:]


def active_gradient_counts(idx, vals, x, n):
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if idx.size == 0:
        return g
    a = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    b = 1.0 - a
    np1 = n - 1
    for t in range(idx.size):
        p = int(idx[t])
        w = float(vals[t])
        if w == 0.0:
            continue
        d = p - np1
        if d >= 0:
            s1 = d
            e1 = n
            if s1 < e1:
                g[s1:e1] += w * b[:e1 - s1]
            s2 = 0
            e2 = n - d
            if s2 < e2:
                g[s2:e2] -= w * a[d:e2 + d]
        else:
            s1 = 0
            e1 = n + d
            if s1 < e1:
                g[s1:e1] += w * b[-d:]
            s2 = -d
            e2 = n
            if s2 < e2:
                g[s2:e2] -= w * a[:e2 - s2]
    return g


def dense_gradient_counts(p, x, n):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).reshape(n)
    if p.size != 2 * n - 1:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    b = 1.0 - x
    g1 = fft_convolve(b, p)[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    g2 = fft_convolve(x[::-1], p)[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
    return g1 - g2


def smooth_objective(x, n, alpha):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    if x.size != n:
        x = x[:n] if x.size > n else np.pad(x, (0, n - x.size))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return 1e100
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c = correlation_counts_fft(x) * dx
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        return 1e100
    m = float(c.max())
    z = alpha * (c - m)
    sh = float(z.max())
    s = float(np.sum(np.exp(z - sh)))
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return float(m)
    return float(m + (sh + math.log(s)) / alpha)


def smooth_gradient(x, n, alpha):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    if x.size != n:
        x = x[:n] if x.size > n else np.pad(x, (0, n - x.size))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c = correlation_counts_fft(x) * dx
    m = float(c.max())
    z = alpha * (c - m)
    sh = float(z.max())
    e = np.exp(z - sh)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    p = e / s
    return dense_gradient_counts(p, x, n) * dx


def _sum_eq(x, n):
    return float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * n)


def _sum_eq_jac(x, n):
    return np.ones(int(n), dtype=float)


def slsqp_smooth(h, n, alpha, maxiter=80):
    h = project_feasible(np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy(), n)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0) for _ in range(n)]
    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': _sum_eq, 'jac': _sum_eq_jac, 'args': (n,)}]
    try:
        res = minimize(
            smooth_objective,
            h,
            args=(n, float(alpha)),
            method='SLSQP',
            jac=smooth_gradient,
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'ftol': 1e-12, 'disp': False}
        )
        return project_feasible(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float), n)
    except Exception:
        return project_feasible(h, n)


def _update_pair_counts(a, brev, c, i, j, delta):
    n = a.shape[0]
    c[i:i + n] += delta * brev
    s = n - 1 - i
    c[s:s + n] -= delta * a

    v = -delta
    c[j:j + n] += v * brev
    s = n - 1 - j
    c[s:s + n] -= v * a

    q = delta * delta
    c[n - 1] -= 2.0 * q
    p = i + n - 1 - j
    if p != n - 1:
        c[p] += q
    p = j + n - 1 - i
    if p != n - 1:
        c[p] += q


def _clamp_pair_state(h, i, j):
    for idx in (int(i), int(j)):
        if h[idx] < 0.0:
            if h[idx] > -1e-12:
                h[idx] = 0.0
    for idx in (int(i), int(j)):
        if h[idx] > 1.0:
            if h[idx] < 1.0 + 1e-12:
                h[idx] = 1.0


def apply_pair_move(h, brev, c, i, j, delta):
    if i == j or abs(delta) < 1e-14:
        return None
    if delta > 0.0:
        m = min(max(0.0, 1.0 - h[i]), max(0.0, h[j]))
        if m <= 1e-13:
            return None
        d = min(delta, m)
    else:
        dm = -delta
        m = min(max(0.0, h[i]), max(0.0, 1.0 - h[j]))
        if m <= 1e-13:
            return None
        d = -min(dm, m)
    if abs(d) < 1e-13:
        return None
    _update_pair_counts(h, brev, c, i, j, d)
    h[i] += d
    h[j] -= d
    _clamp_pair_state(h, i, j)
    n = h.shape[0]
    brev[n - 1 - i] = 1.0 - h[i]
    brev[n - 1 - j] = 1.0 - h[j]
    return d


def revert_pair_move(h, brev, c, i, j, delta):
    if abs(delta) < 1e-14:
        return
    _update_pair_counts(h, brev, c, i, j, -delta)
    h[i] -= delta
    h[j] += delta
    _clamp_pair_state(h, i, j)
    n = h.shape[0]
    brev[n - 1 - i] = 1.0 - h[i]
    brev[n - 1 - j] = 1.0 - h[j]


def subgradient_polish(h, n, deadline, rng, max_iters=25):
    if n < 3:
        return np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy(), evaluate_exact(h)
    h = project_feasible(np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy(), n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    best_c = float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / n))
    best_h = h.copy()
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    stall = 0
    for _ in range(int(max_iters)):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        idx = top_lag_indices(c, 8)
        if idx.size == 0:
            break
        w = np.ones(idx.size, dtype=float) / float(idx.size)
        g = active_gradient_counts(idx, w, h, n) * (2.0 / n)
        d = -g
        d = d - float(d.mean())
        v = 0.7 * v + d
        d = v
        mn = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
        if mn < 1e-14:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 12:
                break
            continue
        d = d / mn
        improved = False
        for step in (0.2, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005, 0.002, 0.001):
            y = project_feasible(h + step * d, n)
            cy = correlation_counts_fft(y)
            val = float(np.max(cy) * (2.0 / n))
            if val < best_c - 1e-11:
                h = y
                c = cy
                best_c = val
                best_h = h.copy()
                v = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
                improved = True
                break
        if improved:
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 12:
                break
    bh = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    bc = evaluate_exact(bh)
    return bh, bc


def fast_pair_polish(h, n, deadline, rng, base_delta=0.02):
    if n < 3:
        h = project_feasible(np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy(), n)
        return h, evaluate_exact(h)

    h = project_feasible(np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy(), n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full').copy()
    brev = (1.0 - h)[::-1].copy()
    best_count = float(np.max(c))
    best_c = best_count * (2.0 / n)
    best_h = h.copy()

    delta = float(base_delta)
    min_delta = 1e-5
    stall = 0
    total = 0
    g = None
    low = None
    high = None
    pair_list = None
    cursor = 0
    epoch_acc = 0
    improve_tol = 1e-8

    while time.time() < deadline - 1.0:
        total += 1

        if total % 1000 == 0:
            c_exact = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
            actual = float(np.max(c_exact))
            if actual < best_count - improve_tol:
                best_count = actual
                best_c = actual * (2.0 / n)
                best_h = h.copy()
            if actual > best_count + 1e-6:
                h = best_h.copy()
            c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full').copy()
            brev = (1.0 - h)[::-1].copy()
            g = None

        if g is None:
            idx = top_lag_indices(c, 10)
            if idx.size > 0:
                w = np.ones(idx.size, dtype=float) / float(idx.size)
                g = active_gradient_counts(idx, w, h, n)
                order = np.argsort(g)
                L = min(40, n // 2)
                low = order[:L]
                high = order[-L:][::-1]
            else:
                g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
                low = np.empty(0, dtype=int)
                high = np.empty(0, dtype=int)

        if pair_list is None or cursor >= len(pair_list):
            plist = []
            if low is not None and high is not None and low.size > 0 and high.size > 0:
                for ii in low:
                    ii = int(ii)
                    for jj in high:
                        jj = int(jj)
                        if ii != jj:
                            plist.append((ii, jj))
                if len(plist) > 400:
                    rng.shuffle(plist)
                    plist = plist[:400]
                else:
                    rng.shuffle(plist)
            if not plist:
                for _ in range(60):
                    ii = int(rng.randint(n))
                    jj = int(rng.randint(n))
                    if ii != jj:
                        plist.append((ii, jj))
            pair_list = plist
            cursor = 0
            epoch_acc = 0

        if pair_list is not None and cursor < len(pair_list) and rng.random() < 0.90:
            i, j = pair_list[cursor]
        else:
            i = int(rng.randint(n))
            j = int(rng.randint(n))
            if i == j:
                j = (j + 1) % n
        cursor += 1

        if g is not None and low is not None and high is not None:
            if g[i] <= g[j]:
                dsign = 1.0
            else:
                dsign = -1.0
            if abs(g[i] - g[j]) < 1e-10:
                dsign = 1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1.0
        else:
            dsign = 1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1.0

        sign_deltas = [dsign * delta]
        if delta > 0.004:
            sign_deltas.append(0.5 * dsign * delta)

        improved_trial = False
        any_feas = False

        for dv in sign_deltas:
            applied = apply_pair_move(h, brev, c, i, j, dv)
            if applied is None:
                continue
            any_feas = True
            new_count = float(np.max(c))
            if new_count < best_count - improve_tol:
                best_count = new_count
                best_c = new_count * (2.0 / n)
                best_h = h.copy()
                improved_trial = True
                epoch_acc += 1
                if epoch_acc >= 8:
                    pair_list = None
                    g = None
                break
            else:
                revert_pair_move(h, brev, c, i, j, applied)

        if not improved_trial and not any_feas:
            for dv in sign_deltas:
                dv = -dv
                applied = apply_pair_move(h, brev, c, i, j, dv)
                if applied is None:
                    continue
                any_feas = True
                new_count = float(np.max(c))
                if new_count < best_count - improve_tol:
                    best_count = new_count
                    best_c = new_count * (2.0 / n)
                    best_h = h.copy()
                    improved_trial = True
                    epoch_acc += 1
                    if epoch_acc >= 8:
                        pair_list = None
                        g = None
                    break
                else:
                    revert_pair_move(h, brev, c, i, j, applied)

        if improved_trial:
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 20000:
                delta *= 0.5
                stall = 0
                if delta < min_delta:
                    break

        if pair_list is None or cursor >= len(pair_list):
            if epoch_acc == 0:
                delta *= 0.5
                if delta < min_delta:
                    break
            pair_list = None
            epoch_acc = 0
            stall = 0

    bh = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    bc = evaluate_exact(bh)
    ch = project_feasible(h, n)
    cc = evaluate_exact(ch)
    if cc < bc:
        bh, bc = ch, cc
    return bh, bc


def anneal_pair_polish(h, n, deadline, rng):
    if n < 3:
        h = project_feasible(np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy(), n)
        return h, evaluate_exact(h)

    h = project_feasible(np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy(), n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full').copy()
    brev = (1.0 - h)[::-1].copy()
    best_count = float(np.max(c))
    best_c = best_count * (2.0 / n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    current_count = best_count

    t0_news = time.time()
    t_end_c = 1e-6
    t_start_c = 5e-4
    decay_iters = 200000
    it = 0

    while time.time() < deadline - 1.0:
        it += 1
        frac = min(1.0, float(it) / float(decay_iters))
        t_c = t_start_c * (t_end_c / t_start_c) ** frac
        t_count = t_c * (0.5 * n)

        i = int(rng.randint(n))
        j = int(rng.randint(n))
        if i == j:
            continue

        mag = (0.002 + 0.05 * (1.0 - frac)) * (0.5 + rng.random())
        dv = mag if rng.random() < 0.5 else -mag
        applied = apply_pair_move(h, brev, c, i, j, dv)
        if applied is None:
            continue

        new_count = float(np.max(c))
        diff = new_count - current_count
        accept = False
        if diff < 0.0:
            accept = True
        elif t_count > 0.0 and diff < 3.0 * t_count:
            u = rng.random()
            if u > 0.0 and math.log(u) < -diff / t_count:
                accept = True

        if accept:
            current_count = new_count
            if new_count < best_count:
                best_count = new_count
                best_c = new_count * (2.0 / n)
                best_h = h.copy()
        else:
            revert_pair_move(h, brev, c, i, j, applied)

        if it % 2000 == 0:
            c_exact = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
            actual = float(np.max(c_exact))
            if actual < best_count:
                best_count = actual
                best_c = actual * (2.0 / n)
                best_h = h.copy()
            if actual > best_count + 0.5:
                h = best_h.copy()
                c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full').copy()
                brev = (1.0 - h)[::-1].copy()
                current_count = best_count
            else:
                c = c_exact.copy()
                brev = (1.0 - h)[::-1].copy()
                current_count = actual

    bh = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    bc = evaluate_exact(bh)
    return bh, bc


def optimize_resolution(h, n, deadline, rng):
    h = project_feasible(np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy(), n)
    best_c = evaluate_exact(h)
    best_h = h.copy()
    if n < 3:
        return best_h, best_c

    if n <= 350 and time.time() < deadline - 30:
        alloc = min(25.0, max(0.0, deadline - time.time() - 10.0))
        if alloc > 8.0:
            hs, cs = subgradient_polish(h, n, time.time() + alloc, rng, max_iters=25)
            if cs < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_h = hs
                best_c = float(cs)

    if n <= 700 and time.time() < deadline - 25:
        rem = float(deadline - time.time())
        if rem < 35.0:
            sched = []
        elif n <= 350:
            sched = [(200, 70), (1500, 60), (10000, 40)]
        elif n <= 600:
            sched = [(500, 40), (3000, 30)]
        else:
            sched = [(1000, 25)]

        for alpha, mx in sched:
            if time.time() > deadline - 15:
                break
            rem = float(deadline - time.time())
            if rem < 35.0:
                break
            ht = slsqp_smooth(best_h, n, alpha, mx)
            c = evaluate_exact(ht)
            if c < best_c - 1e-13:
                best_h = ht
                best_c = float(c)

    if time.time() < deadline - 3:
        hp, cp = fast_pair_polish(best_h, n, deadline - 3, rng, base_delta=0.02)
        if cp < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_h = hp
            best_c = float(cp)

    return best_h, best_c


def initial_construction(n):
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    h = 0.5 + 0.08 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x) + 0.05 * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * x)
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget_s = max(15.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + budget_s - 15.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))

    best_h = None
    best_c = float('inf')

    try:
        par = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
        npar = par.size
        if npar > 2:
            if (par.min() >= -1e-8 and par.max() <= 1.0 + 1e-8
                    and abs(par.sum() - 0.5 * npar) < 1e-6):
                h0 = np.clip(par, 0.0, 1.0)
                h0 = _fix_sum(h0, npar)
            else:
                h0 = project_feasible(par, npar)
            best_h = h0.copy()
            best_c = evaluate_exact(h0)
    except Exception:
        pass

    if best_h is None or not math.isfinite(best_c):
        n0 = 250
        best_h = initial_construction(n0)
        best_c = evaluate_exact(best_h)

    rem = float(deadline - time.time())
    if rem > 80.0:
        first_alloc = max(80.0, min(500.0, 0.55 * rem))
        first_dead = time.time() + first_alloc
        if first_dead > deadline - 80.0:
            first_dead = deadline - 80.0
        if first_dead > time.time() + 20.0:
            h1, c1 = optimize_resolution(best_h, int(best_h.size), first_dead, rng)
            if math.isfinite(c1) and c1 < best_c:
                best_h = h1
                best_c = float(c1)

    rem = float(deadline - time.time())
    nbest = int(np.asarray(best_h).size)
    if rem > 120.0 and nbest <= 350:
        ntry = 500
        if ntry != nbest:
            alloc = min(320.0, max(0.0, rem - 80.0))
            if alloc > 70.0:
                x_old = (np.arange(nbest, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / nbest)
                x_new = (np.arange(ntry, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / ntry)
                h_try = np.interp(x_new, x_old, best_h)
                h_try = project_feasible(h_try, ntry)
                h2, c2 = optimize_resolution(h_try, ntry, time.time() + alloc, rng)
                if math.isfinite(c2) and c2 < best_c:
                    best_h = h2
                    best_c = float(c2)

    n = int(np.asarray(best_h).size)
    rem = float(deadline - time.time())
    if rem > 25.0:
        pair_alloc = max(10.0, min(120.0, 0.5 * rem))
        pair_dead = time.time() + pair_alloc
        if pair_dead > deadline - 15.0:
            pair_dead = deadline - 15.0
        if pair_dead > time.time() + 5.0:
            hf, cf = fast_pair_polish(best_h, n, pair_dead, rng, base_delta=0.006)
            if math.isfinite(cf) and cf < best_c:
                best_h = hf
                best_c = float(cf)

    rem = float(deadline - time.time())
    if rem > 45.0:
        ha, ca = anneal_pair_polish(best_h, n, deadline - 10.0, rng)
        if math.isfinite(ca) and ca < best_c:
            best_h = ha
            best_c = float(ca)

    h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    h = _fix_sum(h, n)
    c = evaluate_exact(h)

    if not math.isfinite(c) or c > best_c + 1e-10:
        h2 = project_feasible(best_h, n)
        h2 = np.clip(h2, 0.0, 1.0)
        h2 = _fix_sum(h2, n)
        c2 = evaluate_exact(h2)
        if math.isfinite(c2) and c2 < c:
            h, c = h2, c2

    if not math.isfinite(c):
        h = np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float)
        c = evaluate_exact(h)

    return np.asarray(h, dtype=float), float(c), int(n)
```
